//! Pure smooth-scrolling physics.
//!
//! No Win32 types, no I/O, no clocks: time is always passed in. This makes
//! the whole module unit-testable on any OS.
//!
//! Units: distances are "px-equivalent". One wheel notch (120 wheel units)
//! maps to `px_per_notch` px-equivalents, so `units = px * 120 / px_per_notch`.
//! NOTE: incomplete file, supceptible to change

mod axis;
mod easing;
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests;

use axis::AxisState;
pub use easing::Easing;

/// Standard wheel delta of one notch.
pub const WHEEL_DELTA: i32 = 120;
/// Largest `dt` (seconds) accepted by [`Engine::tick`]; survives stalls/sleep.
pub const MAX_DT_S: f64 = 0.1;

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Axis {
    Vertical,
    Horizontal,
}

/// One raw wheel event. `t_us` is a monotonic timestamp in microseconds.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]

Fixing a build configuration conflict with targeted compiler flags.
bash

mkdir -p /home/claude/tools && cat > /home/claude/tools/fakewin.sh <<'EOF'
#!/bin/sh
# Dev-only: makes `cfg(windows)` true for glide + the `windows*` crates so their
# real bindings can be type-checked on Linux (`cargo check` never links).
rustc="$1"; shift
case " $* " in
  *" --crate-name glide "*|*" --crate-name windows "*|*" --crate-name windows_"*|*" --crate-name windows-"*) exec "$rustc" "$@" --cfg windows ;;
  *) exec "$rustc" "$@" ;;
esac
EOF
chmod +x /home/claude/tools/fakewin.sh
cd /home/claude/glide && RUSTC_WRAPPER=/home/claude/tools/fakewin.sh cargo check --target x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu 2>&1 | grep -E "^error" -A7 | head -150; RUSTC_WRAPPER=/home/claude/tools/fakewin.sh cargo check --target x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu 2>&1 | tail -2

#!/bin/sh
# Dev-only: makes `cfg(windows)` true for glide + the `windows*` crates so their
# real bindings can be type-checked on Linux (`cargo check` never links).
rustc="$1"; shift
case " $* " in
  *"--print=cfg"*|*"--print cfg"*) exec "$rustc" "$@" --cfg windows ;;
  *" --crate-name glide "*|*" --crate-name windows "*|*" --crate-name windows_"*|*" --crate-name windows-"*) exec "$rustc" "$@" --cfg windows ;;
  *) exec "$rustc" "$@" ;;
esac
EOF

cat > /home/claude/tools/fakewin.sh <<'EOF'
#!/bin/sh
# Dev-only: makes `cfg(windows)` true for glide + `windows` + `windows_core` so the
# real windows-0.58 bindings can be type-checked on Linux (`cargo check` never links).
rustc="$1"; shift
case " $* " in
  *"--print=cfg"*|*"--print cfg"*) exec "$rustc" "$@" --cfg windows ;;
  *" --crate-name glide "*|*" --crate-name windows "*|*" --crate-name windows_core "*) exec "$rustc" "$@" --cfg windows ;;
  *) exec "$rustc" "$@" ;;
esac
EOF
cd /home/claude/glide && RUSTC_WRAPPER=/home/claude/tools/fakewin.sh cargo check --target x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu > /tmp/check.log 2>&1; grep -cE "^error" /tmp/check.log; grep -E "^error" -A6 /tmp/check.log | head -70

Output

59:        std::os::windows::ffi::OsStringExt::from_wide(self.as_wide())
169:                std::os::windows::ffi::OsStrExt::encode_wide(value),
316:            .eq(std::os::windows::ffi::OsStrExt::encode_wide(other))

    /// Get the contents of this `HSTRING` as a OsString.
    #[cfg(feature = "std")]
    pub fn to_os_string(&self) -> std::ffi::OsString {
        std::os::windows::ffi::OsStringExt::from_wide(self.as_wide())
    }

    /// # Safety
#[cfg(feature = "std")]
impl From<&std::ffi::OsStr> for HSTRING {
    fn from(value: &std::ffi::OsStr) -> Self {
        unsafe {
            Self::from_wide_iter(
                std::os::windows::ffi::OsStrExt::encode_wide(value),
                value.len(),
            )
            .unwrap()
    fn eq(&self, other: &std::ffi::OsStr) -> bool {
        self.as_wide()
            .iter()
            .copied()
            .eq(std::os::windows::ffi::OsStrExt::encode_wide(other))
    }
}

cd /home/claude/tools/patched/windows-strings
python3 - <<'EOF'
import re
p='src/hstring.rs'; s=open(p).read()
s=s.replace("std::os::windows::ffi::OsStringExt::from_wide(self.as_wide())","std::ffi::OsString::from(String::from_utf16_lossy(self.as_wide()))")
s=s.replace("std::os::windows::ffi::OsStrExt::encode_wide(value)","value.to_string_lossy().encode_utf16().collect::<Vec<u16>>().into_iter()")
s=s.replace("std::os::windows::ffi::OsStrExt::encode_wide(other)","other.to_string_lossy().encode_utf16().collect::<Vec<u16>>().into_iter()")
open(p,'w').write(s)
EOF

import re
# ---- axis.rs: honour momentum.min_velocity (was parsed/clamped but never used) ----
p='src/engine/axis.rs'; s=open(p).read()
s=s.replace("""            let alpha = 1.0 - cfg.momentum.friction.powf(dt * 60.0);
            self.decay_remaining(alpha)
        } else if""","""            let alpha = 1.0 - cfg.momentum.friction.powf(dt * 60.0);
            // The decaying motion moves at `lambda * remaining` px/s, so the
            // user's `min_velocity` stop threshold is a remaining distance of
            // `min_velocity / lambda`. Capped so a huge value cannot turn the
            // final flush into a visible jump.
            let lambda = -60.0 * cfg.momentum.friction.ln();
            let stop_px = (cfg.momentum.min_velocity / lambda).clamp(EPS_PX, MAX_STOP_PX);
            self.decay_remaining(alpha, stop_px)
        } else if""")
s=s.replace("""            self.decay_remaining(alpha)
        } else {""","""            self.decay_remaining(alpha, EPS_PX)
        } else {""")
s=s.replace("""    /// Shrink `target_remaining` by `alpha`, flushing the tail once it's
    /// within `EPS_PX` so settling happens in finite time with zero loss.
    /// Returns the px moved this frame and whether motion just settled.
    fn decay_remaining(&mut self, alpha: f64) -> (f64, bool) {
        let mv = self.target_remaining * alpha;
        self.target_remaining -= mv;
        if self.target_remaining.abs() < EPS_PX {""","""    /// Shrink `target_remaining` by `alpha`, flushing the tail once it's
    /// within `stop_px` so settling happens in finite time with zero loss.
    /// Returns the px moved this frame and whether motion just settled.
    fn decay_remaining(&mut self, alpha: f64, stop_px: f64) -> (f64, bool) {
        let mv = self.target_remaining * alpha;
        self.target_remaining -= mv;
        if self.target_remaining.abs() < stop_px {""")
s=s.replace("""const EPS_PX: f64 = 0.3;
""","""const EPS_PX: f64 = 0.3;
/// Upper bound for the momentum stop threshold expressed in px (see `tick`).
const MAX_STOP_PX: f64 = 10.0;
""")
open(p,'w').write(s)

    /// mouse-button press (7.1) without having to inspect the config.

cd /home/claude/glide && sed -i 's/^mod state;$/mod state;\nmod trace;/' src/main.rs && cat >> src/engine/tests.rs <<'EOF'

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// momentum.min_velocity (was accepted by the config but ignored by the engine)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

fn momentum_with_min_velocity(v: f64) -> EngineConfig {
    let m = momentum();
    EngineConfig {
        momentum: MomentumConfig {
            min_velocity: v,
            ..m.momentum
        },
        ..m
    }
}

#[test]
fn min_velocity_ends_momentum_sooner_without_losing_distance() {
    let mut fine = Engine::new(momentum_with_min_velocity(0.5));
    let mut coarse = Engine::new(momentum_with_min_velocity(400.0));
    for e in [&mut fine, &mut coarse] {
        e.on_input(notch(120, Axis::Vertical, 0));
    }
    let a = run_until_idle(&mut fine, 1.0 / 60.0);
    let b = run_until_idle(&mut coarse, 1.0 / 60.0);
    assert!(b.secs < a.secs, "higher threshold stops earlier: {} vs {}", b.secs, a.secs);
    assert!((a.v - 120).abs() <= 1 && (b.v - 120).abs() <= 1, "{} {}", a.v, b.v);
    assert!(a.min_v >= 0 && b.min_v >= 0);
}

#[test]
fn huge_min_velocity_cannot_turn_the_tail_into_a_big_jump() {
    let mut e = Engine::new(momentum_with_min_velocity(1.0e6));
    e.on_input(notch(120, Axis::Vertical, 0));
    let r = run_until_idle(&mut e, 1.0 / 60.0);
    assert!((r.v - 120).abs() <= 1, "total {}", r.v);
    // The tail flush is capped (MAX_STOP_PX), so no single frame carries
    // more than the first, natural frame does.
    let mut e = Engine::new(momentum_with_min_velocity(1.0e6));
    e.on_input(notch(120, Axis::Vertical, 0));
    let first = e.tick(1.0 / 60.0).v_units;
    let r = run_until_idle(&mut e, 1.0 / 60.0);
    assert!(r.max_v <= first, "max {} first {first}", r.max_v);
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 2.9 hardening: randomized sequences over every mode
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

fn random_config(rng: &mut Lcg) -> EngineConfig {
    let easing = Easing::ALL[(rng.next() % 5) as usize];
    let use_momentum = rng.next() % 4 == 0;
    EngineConfig {
        step_size: 20.0 + rng.unit() * 980.0,
        animation_time_ms: 50.0 + rng.unit() * 1950.0,
        easing,
        accel: AccelConfig {
            enabled: rng.next() % 2 == 0,
            max_multiplier: 1.0 + rng.unit() * 9.0,
            window_ms: 20.0 + rng.unit() * 480.0,
        },
        momentum: MomentumConfig {
            enabled: use_momentum,
            friction: 0.5 + rng.unit() * 0.499,
            min_velocity: 0.001 + rng.unit() * 50.0,
        },
        reverse_direction: rng.next() % 2 == 0,
        px_per_notch: 60.0 + rng.unit() * 180.0,
    }
}

#[test]
fn fuzz_random_traffic_never_panics_and_always_settles() {
    let mut rng = Lcg(0xC0FF_EE11);
    for case in 0..300 {
        let mut e = Engine::new(random_config(&mut rng));
        let mut t_us = 0_u64;
        for _ in 0..200 {
            // Interleave inputs (both axes, both signs, sometimes non-multiples
            // of 120 or zero), config swaps and ticks with awkward `dt`s.
            match rng.next() % 6 {
                0 | 1 => {
                    let delta = match rng.next() % 5 {
                        0 => 0,
                        1 => 1 + (rng.next() % 119) as i32,
                        2 => -120 * (1 + (rng.next() % 4) as i32),
                        _ => 120 * (1 + (rng.next() % 4) as i32),
                    };
                    let axis = if rng.next() % 3 == 0 {
                        Axis::Horizontal
                    } else {
                        Axis::Vertical
                    };
                    t_us += (rng.unit() * 200_000.0) as u64;
                    e.on_input(notch(delta, axis, t_us));
                }
                2 => e.set_config(random_config(&mut rng)),
                3 => {
                    let dt = match rng.next() % 4 {
                        0 => f64::NAN,
                        1 => -1.0,
                        2 => 50.0,
                        _ => 0.0,
                    };
                    let _ = e.tick(dt);
                }
                _ => {
                    let _ = e.tick(0.001 + rng.unit() * 0.03);
                }
            }
        }
        let r = run_until_idle(&mut e, 1.0 / 120.0);
        assert!(e.is_idle(), "case {case}: did not settle");
        assert!(r.secs < 10.0, "case {case}: took {} s", r.secs);
    }
}

#[test]
fn fuzz_distance_is_conserved_for_same_direction_notches() {
    // Without reversal or acceleration, N notches must travel exactly
    // N * step_size px-equivalent, whatever the mode/timing/frame rate.
    let mut rng = Lcg(0xD15_7A2C);
    for case in 0..200 {
        let mut cfg = random_config(&mut rng);
        cfg.accel.enabled = false;
        cfg.reverse_direction = false;
        let cfg = cfg.sanitized();
        let mut e = Engine::new(cfg);

        let notches = 1 + (rng.next() % 8) as i64;
        let mut total = 0_i64;
        let mut t_us = 0_u64;
        let mut sent = 0_i64;
        while sent < notches {
            if rng.next() % 3 == 0 {
                t_us += 10_000;
                e.on_input(notch(120, Axis::Vertical, t_us));
                sent += 1;
            }
            total += i64::from(e.tick(0.002 + rng.unit() * 0.02).v_units);
        }
        total += run_until_idle(&mut e, 1.0 / 90.0).v;

        let expected = notches as f64 * cfg.step_size * 120.0 / cfg.px_per_notch;
        assert!(
            (total as f64 - expected).abs() <= 1.5,
            "case {case} ({:?}, momentum {}): {total} vs {expected}",
            cfg.easing,
            cfg.momentum.enabled
        );
    }
}
EOF

cd /home/claude/glide && cat >> src/engine/tests.rs <<'EOF'

#[test]
fn dbg_switch_model_mid_motion() {
    // curve-based -> rate-based while a segment is still in flight
    let mut e = Engine::new(with_easing(Easing::EaseOutCubic));
    e.on_input(notch(120, Axis::Vertical, 0));
    let mut total = i64::from(e.tick(1.0 / 60.0).v_units);
    e.set_config(with_easing(Easing::ExponentialSmoothing));
    let r = run_until_idle(&mut e, 1.0 / 60.0);
    total += r.v;
    eprintln!("cubic->exp: idle={} total={total} secs={}", e.is_idle(), r.secs);
    // rate-based -> curve-based
    let mut e = Engine::new(plain());
    e.on_input(notch(120, Axis::Vertical, 0));
    let mut total = i64::from(e.tick(1.0 / 60.0).v_units);
    e.set_config(with_easing(Easing::EaseOutCubic));
    let r = run_until_idle(&mut e, 1.0 / 60.0);
    total += r.v;
    eprintln!("exp->cubic: idle={} total={total} secs={}", e.is_idle(), r.secs);
}
EOF

mkdir -p /home/claude/tools && cat > /home/claude/tools/sim.py <<'EOF'
"""Faithful Python port of src/engine/{axis,mod,easing}.rs + the fuzz tests (dev-only)."""
import math, sys
WHEEL_DELTA=120; MAX_DT=0.1; EPS=0.3; MAX_STOP=10.0; K=5.0
def sgn(x): return math.copysign(1.0,x)
def clamp(x,lo,hi): return max(lo,min(hi,x))
def cf(x,lo,hi,fb): return clamp(x,lo,hi) if math.isfinite(x) else fb
def rnd(x): return math.copysign(math.floor(abs(x)+0.5),x)
EASE=["linear","ease_out_quad","ease_out_cubic","ease_out_expo","exponential_smoothing"]
def apply(e,t):
    t = clamp(t,0,1) if math.isfinite(t) else 1.0
    if e=="linear": return t
    if e=="ease_out_quad": u=1-t; return 1-u*u
    if e=="ease_out_cubic": u=1-t; return 1-u*u*u
    if e=="ease_out_expo": return 1.0 if t>=1 else 1-2**(-10*t)
    return (1-math.exp(-K*t))/(1-math.exp(-K))
class Cfg:
    def __init__(s,**k):
        s.step=120.;s.anim=300.;s.easing="exponential_smoothing";s.acc_en=True;s.acc_max=4.;s.acc_win=120.
        s.mom_en=False;s.fric=.9;s.minv=.5;s.rev=False;s.ppn=120.
        for a,b in k.items(): setattr(s,a,b)
    def san(s):
        import copy; c=copy.copy(s)
        c.step=cf(c.step,1,10000,120);c.anim=cf(c.anim,1,10000,300);c.acc_max=cf(c.acc_max,1,100,1)
        c.acc_win=cf(c.acc_win,1,5000,120);c.fric=cf(c.fric,.5,.999,.9);c.minv=cf(c.minv,1e-3,1e6,.5);c.ppn=cf(c.ppn,1,10000,120)
        return c
class Axis:
    def __init__(s,fix=False):
        s.rem=0.;s.tot=0.;s.prog=0.;s.el=0.;s.frac=0.;s.last=None;s.mult=1.;s.fix=fix
    def rate(s,c): return c.mom_en or c.easing=="exponential_smoothing"
    def migrate(s,c):
        if not s.fix: return
        if s.rate(c):
            if s.tot!=0.0:
                s.rem+=s.tot*(1-s.prog); s.tot=0.;s.prog=0.;s.el=0.
        else:
            if s.rem!=0.0:
                s.tot=s.rem; s.rem=0.; s.prog=0.; s.el=0.
    def push(s,c,delta,t):
        if delta==0: return
        s.migrate(c)
        if c.acc_en:
            if s.last is None: s.mult=1.
            else:
                iv=(t-s.last)/1000.0
                if iv<=c.acc_win: s.mult=min(s.mult+.5,c.acc_max)
                elif iv>c.acc_win*2: s.mult=1.
        else: s.mult=1.
        s.last=t
        d=(-1. if c.rev else 1.)
        dist=delta/WHEEL_DELTA*c.step*s.mult*d
        if not math.isfinite(dist) or dist==0: return
        if s.rate(c):
            if s.rem!=0 and sgn(dist)!=sgn(s.rem): s.rem=0.
            s.rem+=dist
            if not math.isfinite(s.rem): s.rem=sgn(dist)*1e15
        else:
            sr=s.tot*(1-s.prog)
            s.tot = dist if (sr!=0 and sgn(dist)!=sgn(sr)) else sr+dist
            s.prog=0.;s.el=0.
    def decay(s,alpha,stop):
        mv=s.rem*alpha; s.rem-=mv
        if abs(s.rem)<stop:
            lo=s.rem; s.rem=0.; return mv+lo,True
        return mv,False
    def tick(s,c,dt):
        s.migrate(c)
        if dt<=0: return s.emit(c,0.,False)
        if c.mom_en:
            alpha=1-c.fric**(dt*60)
            lam=-60*math.log(c.fric)
            stop=clamp(c.minv/lam,EPS,MAX_STOP)
            mv,st=s.decay(alpha,stop)
        elif c.easing=="exponential_smoothing":
            tau=(c.anim/1000)/K
            alpha=1.0 if tau<=0 else 1-math.exp(-dt/tau)
            mv,st=s.decay(alpha,EPS)
        else:
            dur=max(c.anim/1000,1e-6); s.el+=dt; t=min(s.el/dur,1.)
            np_=apply(c.easing,t); mv=s.tot*(np_-s.prog); s.prog=np_; st=t>=1.
            if st: s.tot=0.;s.prog=0.;s.el=0.
        return s.emit(c,mv,st)
    def emit(s,c,mv,st):
        if mv==0.0 and s.frac==0.0: return 0
        u=mv*WHEEL_DELTA/c.ppn+s.frac
        w=rnd(u) if st else float(math.trunc(u))
        s.frac=0. if st else u-w
        return int(clamp(w,-2**31,2**31-1))
    def settled(s): return abs(s.rem)<EPS and abs(s.tot)<EPS
class Engine:
    def __init__(s,c,fix=False): s.c=c.san(); s.v=Axis(fix); s.h=Axis(fix)
    def set_config(s,c): s.c=c.san()
    def on_input(s,delta,axis,t): (s.v if axis=="v" else s.h).push(s.c,delta,t)
    def tick(s,dt):
        dt=clamp(dt,0,MAX_DT) if math.isfinite(dt) else 0.
        return s.v.tick(s.c,dt),s.h.tick(s.c,dt)
    def idle(s): return s.v.settled() and s.h.settled()
def run_until_idle(e,dt,cap=10.0):
    v=h=0;secs=0.;mx=0;mn=0
    for _ in range(int(cap/dt)):
        if e.idle(): break
        a,b=e.tick(dt); v+=a;h+=b;mn=min(mn,a);mx=max(mx,a);secs+=dt
    return dict(v=v,h=h,secs=secs,min_v=mn,max_v=mx)
class Lcg:
    def __init__(s,x): s.x=x
    def next(s):
        s.x=(s.x*6364136223846793005+1442695040888963407)&(2**64-1); return s.x>>33
    def unit(s): return (s.next()%10000)/10000
def random_config(r):
    easing=EASE[r.next()%5]; use_m=r.next()%4==0
    c=Cfg(); c.step=20+r.unit()*980; c.anim=50+r.unit()*1950; c.easing=easing
    c.acc_en=r.next()%2==0; c.acc_max=1+r.unit()*9; c.acc_win=20+r.unit()*480
    c.mom_en=use_m; c.fric=.5+r.unit()*.499; c.minv=.001+r.unit()*50
    c.rev=r.next()%2==0; c.ppn=60+r.unit()*180
    return c
EOF

import sys; sys.path.insert(0,'/home/claude/tools')
from sim import *
def fuzz_traffic(fix,max_fric=None,cap=10.0):
    r=Lcg(0xC0FFEE11); bad=[]
    for case in range(300):
        c=random_config(r)
        if max_fric and c.mom_en: c.fric=min(c.fric,max_fric)
        e=Engine(c,fix); t=0
        for _ in range(200):
            m=r.next()%6
            if m in(0,1):
                k=r.next()%5
                d=0 if k==0 else (1+r.next()%119 if k==1 else (-120*(1+r.next()%4) if k==2 else 120*(1+r.next()%4)))
                ax="h" if r.next()%3==0 else "v"
                t+=int(r.unit()*200000); e.on_input(d,ax,t)
            elif m==2:
                nc=random_config(r)
                if max_fric and nc.mom_en: nc.fric=min(nc.fric,max_fric)
                e.set_config(nc)
            elif m==3:
                k=r.next()%4; e.tick([float('nan'),-1.0,50.0,0.0][k])
            else: e.tick(0.001+r.unit()*0.03)
        res=run_until_idle(e,1/120,cap)
        if not e.idle() or res['secs']>=cap: bad.append((case,e.idle(),round(res['secs'],2),c.mom_en,round(c.fric,3)))
    return bad
def fuzz_dist(fix,cases=200):
    r=Lcg(0xD157A2C); bad=[]
    for case in range(cases):
        c=random_config(r); c.acc_en=False; c.rev=False; c=c.san(); e=Engine(c,fix)
        n=1+r.next()%8; tot=0; t=0; sent=0
        while sent<n:
            if r.next()%3==0: t+=10000; e.on_input(120,"v",t); sent+=1
            tot+=e.tick(0.002+r.unit()*0.02)[0]
        tot+=run_until_idle(e,1/90,60.0)['v']
        exp=n*c.step*120/c.ppn
        if abs(tot-exp)>1.5: bad.append((case,c.easing,c.mom_en,round(c.fric,3),round(c.minv,2),tot,round(exp,1)))
    return bad
if __name__=="__main__":
    fix = len(sys.argv)>1 and sys.argv[1]=="fix"
    print("FIX" if fix else "CURRENT")
    b=fuzz_traffic(fix); print("traffic: not-settled cases:",len(b),b[:4])
    b=fuzz_dist(fix); print("distance: bad cases:",len(b)); [print("  ",x) for x in b[:6]]

/// Like [`run_until_idle`] with an explicit safety cap in seconds (a very
/// high momentum `friction` legitimately glides for tens of seconds).
fn run_until_idle_for(e: &mut Engine, dt: f64, cap_secs: f64) -> Run {
    let mut r = Run {
        v: 0,
        h: 0,
        secs: 0.0,
        min_v: 0,
        max_v: 0,
    };
    let max_ticks = (cap_secs / dt) as usize;"""

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// momentum.min_velocity (was accepted by the config but ignored by the engine)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

fn momentum_with_min_velocity(v: f64) -> EngineConfig {
    let m = momentum();
    EngineConfig {
        momentum: MomentumConfig {
            min_velocity: v,
            ..m.momentum
        },
        ..m
    }
}

#[test]
fn min_velocity_ends_momentum_sooner_without_losing_distance() {
    let mut fine = Engine::new(momentum_with_min_velocity(0.5));
    let mut coarse = Engine::new(momentum_with_min_velocity(400.0));
    for e in [&mut fine, &mut coarse] {
        e.on_input(notch(120, Axis::Vertical, 0));
    }
    let a = run_until_idle(&mut fine, 1.0 / 60.0);
    let b = run_until_idle(&mut coarse, 1.0 / 60.0);
    assert!(
        b.secs < a.secs,
        "higher threshold stops earlier: {} vs {}",
        b.secs,
        a.secs
    );
    assert!((a.v - 120).abs() <= 1 && (b.v - 120).abs() <= 1, "{} {}", a.v, b.v);
    assert!(a.min_v >= 0 && b.min_v >= 0);
}

#[test]
fn huge_min_velocity_cannot_turn_the_tail_into_a_big_jump() {
    let mut e = Engine::new(momentum_with_min_velocity(1.0e6));
    e.on_input(notch(120, Axis::Vertical, 0));
    let first = e.tick(1.0 / 60.0).v_units;
    let r = run_until_idle(&mut e, 1.0 / 60.0);
    assert!(
        (i64::from(first) + r.v - 120).abs() <= 1,
        "total {}",
        i64::from(first) + r.v
    );
    // The tail flush is capped (MAX_STOP_PX), so no single frame carries
    // more than the first, natural frame does.
    assert!(r.max_v <= first, "max {} first {first}", r.max_v);
}

#[test]

    cfg.momentum.friction = 0.999;
    let mut e = Engine::new(cfg);
    e.on_input(notch(120, Axis::Vertical, 0));

    assert!(e.is_idle(), "settled after {} s", r.secs);

// Live reload can switch the accumulation model while motion is in flight.

#[test]
fn switching_model_mid_motion_neither_strands_nor_loses_distance() {
    let models = [
        with_easing(Easing::Linear),
        with_easing(Easing::EaseOutQuad),
        with_easing(Easing::EaseOutCubic),
        with_easing(Easing::EaseOutExpo),
        with_easing(Easing::ExponentialSmoothing),
        momentum(),
    ];
    for (i, from) in models.iter().enumerate() {
        for (j, to) in models.iter().enumerate() {
            let mut e = Engine::new(*from);
            for k in 0..3 {
                e.on_input(notch(120, Axis::Vertical, k * 20_000));
            }
            let mut total = 0_i64;
            for _ in 0..4 {
                total += i64::from(e.tick(1.0 / 60.0).v_units);
            }
            e.set_config(*to);
            e.on_input(notch(120, Axis::Vertical, 200_000));
            let r = run_until_idle_for(&mut e, 1.0 / 60.0, 60.0);
            total += r.v;
            assert!(e.is_idle(), "{i}->{j}: stranded motion");
            assert!((total - 480).abs() <= 1, "{i}->{j}: total {total}");
        }
    }
}

#[test]
fn reversal_right_after_a_model_switch_cancels_the_old_motion() {
    let mut e = Engine::new(with_easing(Easing::EaseOutCubic));
    e.on_input(notch(120, Axis::Vertical, 0));
    e.tick(1.0 / 60.0);
    e.set_config(plain());
    e.on_input(notch(-120, Axis::Vertical, 50_000));
    let first = e.tick(1.0 / 60.0).v_units;
    assert!(first < 0, "immediate opposite motion");
    let r = run_until_idle(&mut e, 1.0 / 60.0);
    assert!(r.max_v <= 0, "no forward residue after the switch");
    assert!((i64::from(first) + r.v + 120).abs() <= 1);
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 2.9 hardening: randomized sequences over every mode
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

/// `max_friction` bounds momentum glide time so the fixed 10 s test cap stays
/// meaningful (very high friction has its own dedicated test above).
fn random_config(rng: &mut Lcg, max_friction: f64) -> EngineConfig {
    let easing = Easing::ALL[(rng.next() % 5) as usize];
    let use_momentum = rng.next() % 4 == 0;
    let mut cfg = EngineConfig {
        step_size: 20.0 + rng.unit() * 980.0,
        animation_time_ms: 50.0 + rng.unit() * 1950.0,
        easing,
        accel: AccelConfig {
            enabled: rng.next() % 2 == 0,
            max_multiplier: 1.0 + rng.unit() * 9.0,
            window_ms: 20.0 + rng.unit() * 480.0,
        },
        momentum: MomentumConfig {
            enabled: use_momentum,
            friction: 0.5 + rng.unit() * 0.499,
            min_velocity: 0.001 + rng.unit() * 50.0,
        },
        reverse_direction: rng.next() % 2 == 0,
        px_per_notch: 60.0 + rng.unit() * 180.0,
    };
    cfg.momentum.friction = cfg.momentum.friction.min(max_friction);
    cfg
}

#[test]
fn fuzz_random_traffic_never_panics_and_always_settles() {
    let mut rng = Lcg(0xC0FF_EE11);
    for case in 0..300 {
        let mut e = Engine::new(random_config(&mut rng, 0.95));
        let mut t_us = 0_u64;
        for _ in 0..200 {
            // Interleave inputs (both axes, both signs, sometimes non-multiples
            // of 120 or zero), config swaps and ticks with awkward `dt`s.
            match rng.next() % 6 {
                0 | 1 => {
                    let delta = match rng.next() % 5 {
                        0 => 0,
                        1 => 1 + (rng.next() % 119) as i32,
                        2 => -120 * (1 + (rng.next() % 4) as i32),
                        _ => 120 * (1 + (rng.next() % 4) as i32),
                    };
                    let axis = if rng.next() % 3 == 0 {
                        Axis::Horizontal
                    } else {
                        Axis::Vertical
                    };
                    t_us += (rng.unit() * 200_000.0) as u64;
                    e.on_input(notch(delta, axis, t_us));
                }
                2 => e.set_config(random_config(&mut rng, 0.95)),
                3 => {
                    let dt = match rng.next() % 4 {
                        0 => f64::NAN,
                        1 => -1.0,
                        2 => 50.0,
                        _ => 0.0,
                    };
                    let _ = e.tick(dt);
                }
                _ => {
                    let _ = e.tick(0.001 + rng.unit() * 0.03);
                }
            }
        }
        let r = run_until_idle(&mut e, 1.0 / 120.0);
        assert!(e.is_idle(), "case {case}: did not settle");
        assert!(r.secs < 10.0, "case {case}: took {} s", r.secs);
    }
}

#[test]
fn fuzz_distance_is_conserved_for_same_direction_notches() {
    // Without reversal or acceleration, N notches must travel exactly
    // N * step_size px-equivalent, whatever the mode/timing/frame rate.
    let mut rng = Lcg(0xD15_7A2C);
    for case in 0..200 {
        let mut cfg = random_config(&mut rng, 0.999);
        cfg.accel.enabled = false;
        cfg.reverse_direction = false;
        let cfg = cfg.sanitized();
        let mut e = Engine::new(cfg);

        let notches = 1 + (rng.next() % 8) as i64;
        let mut total = 0_i64;
        let mut t_us = 0_u64;
        let mut sent = 0_i64;
        while sent < notches {
            if rng.next() % 3 == 0 {
                t_us += 10_000;
                e.on_input(notch(120, Axis::Vertical, t_us));
                sent += 1;
            }
            total += i64::from(e.tick(0.002 + rng.unit() * 0.02).v_units);
        }
        // Friction up to 0.999 may glide for tens of seconds: generous cap.
        total += run_until_idle_for(&mut e, 1.0 / 90.0, 600.0).v;

        let expected = notches as f64 * cfg.step_size * 120.0 / cfg.px_per_notch;
        assert!(
            (total as f64 - expected).abs() <= 1.5,
            "case {case} ({:?}, momentum {}): {total} vs {expected}",
            cfg.easing,
            cfg.momentum.enabled
        );
    }
}

cd /home/claude/glide/src/platform/win32 && cat > hook.rs <<'EOF'
//! Low-level mouse hook (1.1) + the pass-through rules that are cheap enough
//! to run in the hook callback (CLAUDE.md §7.4). Everything expensive
//! (config snapshot, window lookup) is a cache hit in the common case.
//!
//! The callback must return in well under the ~300 ms `LowLevelHooksTimeout`
//! (target: p99 < 100 µs, NFR table) or Windows silently unhooks it, so the
//! swallow path never allocates and the window lookup is TTL-cached (a cache
//! *miss* allocates one short string, at most once per window / 500 ms).

use super::timer::boost_thread_priority;
use super::window::WindowCache;
use super::EXTRA_INFO_TAG;
use crate::config::Modifier;
use crate::engine::{Axis, WheelInput, WHEEL_DELTA};
use crate::state::{QueuedInput, Shared};
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicU32, AtomicU64, Ordering};
use std::sync::{Arc, OnceLock};
use std::time::Instant;
use windows::Win32::Foundation::{HINSTANCE, LPARAM, LRESULT, WPARAM};
use windows::Win32::System::Threading::GetCurrentThreadId;
use windows::Win32::UI::Input::KeyboardAndMouse::{
    GetAsyncKeyState, VIRTUAL_KEY, VK_CONTROL, VK_LWIN, VK_MENU, VK_RWIN, VK_SHIFT,
};
use windows::Win32::UI::WindowsAndMessaging::{
    CallNextHookEx, DispatchMessageW, GetMessageW, PostThreadMessageW, SetWindowsHookExW,
    TranslateMessage, UnhookWindowsHookEx, HHOOK, LLMHF_INJECTED, LLMHF_LOWER_IL_INJECTED, MSG,
    MSLLHOOKSTRUCT, WH_MOUSE_LL, WM_APP, WM_LBUTTONDOWN, WM_MBUTTONDOWN, WM_MOUSEHWHEEL,
    WM_MOUSEWHEEL, WM_QUIT, WM_RBUTTONDOWN, WM_XBUTTONDOWN,
};

/// The one hook instance this process installs; only the hook thread reads it.
static SHARED: OnceLock<Arc<Shared>> = OnceLock::new();
/// So `stop()`/`reinstall()` (called from the UI thread) can reach the hook
/// thread's `GetMessageW` loop.
static HOOK_THREAD_ID: AtomicU32 = AtomicU32::new(0);
/// Monotonic clock for wheel timestamps (acceleration cadence, FR-6).
static START: OnceLock<Instant> = OnceLock::new();
/// Rate limit for `reinstall` (ms on the `START` clock; 0 = never).
static LAST_REINSTALL_MS: AtomicU64 = AtomicU64::new(0);

const WM_REINSTALL: u32 = WM_APP + 2;
const MIN_REINSTALL_GAP_MS: u64 = 2_000;

/// Microseconds since the first call. No allocation, no syscall beyond QPC.
fn now_us() -> u64 {
    START.get_or_init(Instant::now).elapsed().as_micros() as u64
}

fn key_down(vk: VIRTUAL_KEY) -> bool {
    // SAFETY: `GetAsyncKeyState` takes a plain virtual-key code; no pointers.
    (unsafe { GetAsyncKeyState(i32::from(vk.0)) } as u16 & 0x8000) != 0
}

fn modifier_held(m: Modifier) -> bool {
    match m {
        Modifier::None => false,
        Modifier::Alt => key_down(VK_MENU),
        Modifier::Ctrl => key_down(VK_CONTROL),
        Modifier::Shift => key_down(VK_SHIFT),
        Modifier::Win => key_down(VK_LWIN) || key_down(VK_RWIN),
    }
}

thread_local! {
    static WINDOW_CACHE: std::cell::RefCell<WindowCache> = std::cell::RefCell::new(WindowCache::new());
}

/// Runs entirely inside the OS callback: decide swallow-and-queue vs.
/// pass-through. Returns `true` to swallow the event.
fn handle_wheel(msg: u32, ll: &MSLLHOOKSTRUCT) -> bool {
    // Rule 1: never re-process injected input. Our own output is always
    // injected (and tagged); events injected by other tools (touchpad
    // drivers, remote sessions, mouse software) are already "smooth" or
    // deliberate, so they pass through untouched too.
    if ll.flags & (LLMHF_INJECTED | LLMHF_LOWER_IL_INJECTED) != 0 || ll.dwExtraInfo == EXTRA_INFO_TAG
    {
        return false;
    }

    let Some(shared) = SHARED.get() else {
        return false;
    };

    // Rule 2: disabled/paused.
    if !shared.enabled() {
        return false;
    }

    // The signed wheel delta lives in the high word of `mouseData`.
    let delta = ((ll.mouseData >> 16) & 0xFFFF) as i16 as i32;

    // Rule 3: precision devices emit deltas that aren't multiples of 120.
    if delta == 0 || delta % WHEEL_DELTA != 0 {
        return false;
    }

    let (_, cfg) = shared.snapshot();

    // Rule 4: bypass modifier held.
    if modifier_held(cfg.bypass_modifier) {
        return false;
    }

    // Rule 6: Ctrl+wheel (zoom) stays native.
    let vertical_msg = msg == WM_MOUSEWHEEL;
    if vertical_msg && cfg.ctrl_passthrough && key_down(VK_CONTROL) {
        return false;
    }

    // Rule 5: per-app exclude/allowlist, UIPI-blocked (elevated) targets.
    let (allowed, profile, uipi_blocked) = WINDOW_CACHE.with(|c| {
        let mut cache = c.borrow_mut();
        let target = cache.target_under_cursor();
        (
            target.exe.is_empty() || cfg.app_allowed(&target.exe),
            cfg.profile_for(&target.exe),
            target.uipi_blocked,
        )
    });
    if !allowed || uipi_blocked {
        return false;
    }
    if cfg.auto_disable_in_fullscreen && shared.fullscreen() {
        return false;
    }

    let settings = cfg.settings_for_profile(profile);
    if !settings.enabled {
        return false;
    }

    let (axis, delta) = if vertical_msg {
        // FR-11: Shift+wheel scrolls horizontally. Wheel-down (negative)
        // must move content right (positive `HWHEEL`), hence the sign flip.
        if settings.shift_horizontal && settings.horizontal_enabled && key_down(VK_SHIFT) {
            (Axis::Horizontal, -delta)
        } else {
            (Axis::Vertical, delta)
        }
    } else if settings.horizontal_enabled {
        (Axis::Horizontal, delta)
    } else {
        return false;
    };

    let queued = QueuedInput {
        input: WheelInput {
            delta,
            axis,
            t_us: now_us(),
        },
        profile,
    };
    if shared.queue.push(queued) {
        shared.wake();
    }
    // Full or not, this event has been claimed: never let it double-scroll.
    true
}

fn is_button_down(msg: u32) -> bool {
    msg == WM_LBUTTONDOWN || msg == WM_RBUTTONDOWN || msg == WM_MBUTTONDOWN || msg == WM_XBUTTONDOWN
}

/// The actual `WH_MOUSE_LL` callback. Must be `extern "system"` and return
/// promptly; all real logic lives in `handle_wheel` above.
unsafe extern "system" fn low_level_mouse_proc(code: i32, wparam: WPARAM, lparam: LPARAM) -> LRESULT {
    if code >= 0 {
        let msg = wparam.0 as u32;
        if msg == WM_MOUSEWHEEL || msg == WM_MOUSEHWHEEL {
            // SAFETY: for `code >= 0`, Windows guarantees `lparam` points to a
            // valid `MSLLHOOKSTRUCT` for the duration of this callback.
            let ll = unsafe { &*(lparam.0 as *const MSLLHOOKSTRUCT) };
            if handle_wheel(msg, ll) {
                return LRESULT(1);
            }
        } else if is_button_down(msg) {
            // 7.1: a click stops tablet-style inertia (never swallowed).
            if let Some(shared) = SHARED.get() {
                if shared.cancel_on_click() {
                    shared.request_cancel();
                }
            }
        }
    }
    // SAFETY: `code`/`wparam`/`lparam` are passed through unmodified, and
    // the hook handle argument is ignored for low-level hooks.
    unsafe { CallNextHookEx(HHOOK::default(), code, wparam, lparam) }
}

fn install() -> Option<HHOOK> {
    // SAFETY: `low_level_mouse_proc` is `extern "system"`, matches the
    // `HOOKPROC` signature, and stays valid for the process lifetime (it's a
    // plain `fn`, not a closure). A null module + thread id 0 is the
    // documented way to install a *global* low-level hook.
    unsafe { SetWindowsHookExW(WH_MOUSE_LL, Some(low_level_mouse_proc), HINSTANCE::default(), 0) }.ok()
}

/// Runs the hook on the calling thread until [`stop`] is called (or the
/// process is torn down). The hook is always removed before returning.
pub fn run(shared: Arc<Shared>) -> Result<(), String> {
    let _ = START.get_or_init(Instant::now);
    let _ = SHARED.set(shared);
    boost_thread_priority();
    // SAFETY: `GetCurrentThreadId` has no preconditions.
    HOOK_THREAD_ID.store(unsafe { GetCurrentThreadId() }, Ordering::Release);

    let Some(mut hook) = install() else {
        HOOK_THREAD_ID.store(0, Ordering::Release);
        return Err("could not install the mouse hook (SetWindowsHookExW failed)".to_owned());
    };

    let mut msg = MSG::default();
    // SAFETY: `GetMessageW`/`TranslateMessage`/`DispatchMessageW` on a
    // stack-local `MSG` is the standard Win32 message-loop pattern; `hook`
    // is a live handle we own and unhook exactly once per replacement.
    unsafe {
        loop {
            let r = GetMessageW(&mut msg, None, 0, 0);
            if r.0 <= 0 {
                break; // 0 = WM_QUIT, -1 = error
            }
            if msg.message == WM_REINSTALL {
                // FR-22: Windows may have dropped the hook (timeout, session
                // switch, resume). Install the new one *before* removing the
                // old so there is no unhooked gap.
                if let Some(fresh) = install() {
                    let _ = UnhookWindowsHookEx(hook);
                    hook = fresh;
                }
                continue;
            }
            let _ = TranslateMessage(&msg);
            DispatchMessageW(&msg);
        }
        // Always unhook, on both `WM_QUIT` and message-loop errors.
        let _ = UnhookWindowsHookEx(hook);
    }
    HOOK_THREAD_ID.store(0, Ordering::Release);
    Ok(())
}

fn post_to_hook_thread(msg: u32) {
    let tid = HOOK_THREAD_ID.load(Ordering::Acquire);
    if tid != 0 {
        // SAFETY: posting a message to a thread id we obtained ourselves; if
        // the thread has already exited this is a documented no-op error.
        let _ = unsafe { PostThreadMessageW(tid, msg, WPARAM(0), LPARAM(0)) };
    }
}

/// Ask the hook thread's message loop to exit (FR-22: hook is always
/// removed on shutdown).
pub fn stop() {
    post_to_hook_thread(WM_QUIT);
}

/// Ask the hook thread to re-install the hook (after unlock / resume /
/// remote-session changes). Rate-limited so event storms cannot thrash it.
pub fn reinstall() {
    let now_ms = (now_us() / 1_000).max(1);
    let last = LAST_REINSTALL_MS.load(Ordering::Relaxed);
    if last != 0 && now_ms.saturating_sub(last) < MIN_REINSTALL_GAP_MS {
        return;
    }
    LAST_REINSTALL_MS.store(now_ms, Ordering::Relaxed);
    post_to_hook_thread(WM_REINSTALL);
}

//! Cursor -> root window -> PID -> exe name / elevation, with a tiny cache so
//! the hook's fast path rarely pays for a real lookup (6.2, 6.3). Also the
//! foreground-fullscreen heuristic for FR-21.

use std::ffi::c_void;
use std::sync::OnceLock;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};
use windows::core::PWSTR;
use windows::Win32::Foundation::{CloseHandle, HANDLE, HWND};
use windows::Win32::Graphics::Gdi::{
    GetMonitorInfoW, MonitorFromWindow, MONITORINFO, MONITOR_DEFAULTTONEAREST,
};
use windows::Win32::Security::{GetTokenInformation, TokenElevation, TOKEN_ELEVATION, TOKEN_QUERY};
use windows::Win32::System::Threading::{
    GetCurrentProcess, OpenProcess, OpenProcessToken, QueryFullProcessImageNameW,
    PROCESS_NAME_WIN32, PROCESS_QUERY_INFORMATION, PROCESS_QUERY_LIMITED_INFORMATION,
};
use windows::Win32::UI::WindowsAndMessaging::{
    GetAncestor, GetClassNameW, GetCursorPos, GetDesktopWindow, GetForegroundWindow,
    GetShellWindow, GetWindowLongPtrW, GetWindowRect, GetWindowThreadProcessId, WindowFromPoint,
    GA_ROOT, GWL_STYLE, WS_CAPTION,
};

/// exe lookups are relatively expensive (process open + a few syscalls); the
/// cursor usually sits over the same window for many consecutive wheel
/// events, so remember the last answer for a short TTL.
const CACHE_TTL: Duration = Duration::from_millis(500);

/// Windows where borderless "fullscreen" is a normal, non-game state: video
/// players, browsers (F11 / video fullscreen) and presenters keep smoothing.
const FULLSCREEN_EXEMPT: [&str; 14] = [
    "chrome.exe",
    "msedge.exe",
    "firefox.exe",
    "brave.exe",
    "opera.exe",
    "vivaldi.exe",
    "vlc.exe",
    "mpc-hc.exe",
    "mpc-hc64.exe",
    "mpv.exe",
    "potplayermini64.exe",
    "wmplayer.exe",
    "powerpnt.exe",
    "sumatrapdf.exe",
];

/// What the hook needs to know about the window under the cursor.
pub struct Target {
    /// Lowercase exe name (`""` if unknown).
    pub exe: String,
    /// The target is elevated (or protected) while Glide is not: Windows
    /// (UIPI) would drop our injected wheel events, so we must pass the real
    /// ones through instead of swallowing them ("dead scroll").
    pub uipi_blocked: bool,
}

impl Target {
    fn unknown() -> Self {
        Target {
            exe: String::new(),
            uipi_blocked: false,
        }
    }
}

pub struct WindowCache {
    hwnd: isize,
    target: Target,
    checked_at: Instant,
}

impl WindowCache {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        WindowCache {
            hwnd: 0,
            target: Target::unknown(),
            checked_at: Instant::now() - CACHE_TTL - Duration::from_secs(1),
        }
    }

    pub fn target_under_cursor(&mut self) -> &Target {
        let Some(hwnd) = root_window_under_cursor() else {
            self.hwnd = 0;
            self.target = Target::unknown();
            return &self.target;
        };
        let now = Instant::now();
        let key = hwnd.0 as isize;
        if key == self.hwnd && now.duration_since(self.checked_at) < CACHE_TTL {
            return &self.target;
        }
        self.hwnd = key;
        self.checked_at = now;
        self.target = inspect(hwnd);
        &self.target
    }
}

impl Default for WindowCache {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

fn root_window_under_cursor() -> Option<HWND> {
    // SAFETY: `GetCursorPos`/`WindowFromPoint`/`GetAncestor` take no
    // invariants beyond a valid out-pointer, which `point` provides.
    unsafe {
        let mut point = Default::default();
        if GetCursorPos(&mut point).is_err() {
            return None;
        }
        let leaf = WindowFromPoint(point);
        if leaf.0.is_null() {
            return None;
        }
        let root = GetAncestor(leaf, GA_ROOT);
        Some(if root.0.is_null() { leaf } else { root })
    }
}

/// Is the token behind `process` elevated? `None` if it can't be read.
fn token_elevated(process: HANDLE) -> Option<bool> {
    // SAFETY: `token` is an out-handle we close before returning; the
    // `TOKEN_ELEVATION` buffer and its size match what
    // `GetTokenInformation(TokenElevation)` writes.
    unsafe {
        let mut token = HANDLE::default();
        OpenProcessToken(process, TOKEN_QUERY, &mut token).ok()?;
        let mut elevation = TOKEN_ELEVATION::default();
        let mut returned = 0u32;
        let result = GetTokenInformation(
            token,
            TokenElevation,
            Some((&mut elevation as *mut TOKEN_ELEVATION).cast::<c_void>()),
            std::mem::size_of::<TOKEN_ELEVATION>() as u32,
            &mut returned,
        );
        let _ = CloseHandle(token);
        result.ok()?;
        Some(elevation.TokenIsElevated != 0)
    }
}

fn self_elevated() -> bool {
    static SELF: OnceLock<bool> = OnceLock::new();
    *SELF.get_or_init(|| {
        // SAFETY: `GetCurrentProcess` returns a pseudo-handle (no cleanup).
        token_elevated(unsafe { GetCurrentProcess() }).unwrap_or(false)
    })
}

fn image_name(process: HANDLE) -> Option<String> {
    let mut buf = [0u16; 1024];
    let mut len = buf.len() as u32;
    // SAFETY: `buf`/`len` describe a valid writable UTF-16 buffer that
    // outlives the call.
    let ok = unsafe {
        QueryFullProcessImageNameW(process, PROCESS_NAME_WIN32, PWSTR(buf.as_mut_ptr()), &mut len)
    }
    .is_ok();
    if !ok || len == 0 {
        return None;
    }
    let path = String::from_utf16_lossy(&buf[..len as usize]);
    path.rsplit(['\\', '/'])
        .next()
        .map(|s| s.to_ascii_lowercase())
}

fn window_pid(hwnd: HWND) -> u32 {
    let mut pid = 0u32;
    // SAFETY: `hwnd` is a window handle we just obtained; `pid` is a valid
    // out-pointer. A stale handle simply yields pid 0.
    unsafe { GetWindowThreadProcessId(hwnd, Some(&mut pid)) };
    pid
}

fn inspect(hwnd: HWND) -> Target {
    let pid = window_pid(hwnd);
    if pid == 0 {
        return Target::unknown();
    }
    let own_elevated = self_elevated();
    // SAFETY: the process handles opened here are owned locally and closed
    // before return.
    unsafe {
        let Ok(process) = OpenProcess(PROCESS_QUERY_LIMITED_INFORMATION, false, pid) else {
            // Protected / other-session process: we cannot even query it, so
            // injected input would not reach it either.
            return Target {
                exe: String::new(),
                uipi_blocked: !own_elevated,
            };
        };
        let exe = image_name(process).unwrap_or_default();
        let target_elevated = match token_elevated(process) {
            Some(e) => e,
            // Token unreadable with a limited handle: an elevated process
            // refuses a fuller handle, a normal one grants it.
            None => match OpenProcess(PROCESS_QUERY_INFORMATION, false, pid) {
                Ok(full) => {
                    let _ = CloseHandle(full);
                    false
                }
                Err(_) => true,
            },
        };
        let _ = CloseHandle(process);
        Target {
            exe,
            uipi_blocked: target_elevated && !own_elevated,
        }
    }
}

fn exe_of_window(hwnd: HWND) -> Option<String> {
    let pid = window_pid(hwnd);
    if pid == 0 {
        return None;
    }
    // SAFETY: the process handle is owned locally and closed before return.
    unsafe {
        let process = OpenProcess(PROCESS_QUERY_LIMITED_INFORMATION, false, pid).ok()?;
        let name = image_name(process);
        let _ = CloseHandle(process);
        name
    }
}

/// True when the foreground window looks like an exclusive/borderless
/// *game*: no title bar, covering its whole monitor, not the shell/desktop
/// and not a browser/video-player/presenter (whose fullscreen keeps scrolling).
pub fn foreground_is_fullscreen() -> bool {
    // SAFETY: all calls take plain handles/out-pointers we own; no lifetime
    // requirements beyond this function's scope.
    unsafe {
        let hwnd = GetForegroundWindow();
        if hwnd.0.is_null() || hwnd == GetShellWindow() || hwnd == GetDesktopWindow() {
            return false;
        }

        let mut class = [0u16; 64];
        let n = GetClassNameW(hwnd, &mut class);
        let class = String::from_utf16_lossy(&class[..n.max(0) as usize]);
        if class == "Progman" || class == "WorkerW" {
            return false; // the desktop itself
        }

        // A title bar means a normal (even maximized) window.
        let style = GetWindowLongPtrW(hwnd, GWL_STYLE) as u32;
        if style & WS_CAPTION.0 == WS_CAPTION.0 {
            return false;
        }

        let mut rect = Default::default();
        if GetWindowRect(hwnd, &mut rect).is_err() {
            return false;
        }
        let monitor = MonitorFromWindow(hwnd, MONITOR_DEFAULTTONEAREST);
        let mut info = MONITORINFO {
            cbSize: std::mem::size_of::<MONITORINFO>() as u32,
            ..Default::default()
        };
        if !GetMonitorInfoW(monitor, &mut info).as_bool() {
            return false;
        }
        let m = info.rcMonitor;
        let covers = rect.left <= m.left && rect.top <= m.top && rect.right >= m.right && rect.bottom >= m.bottom;
        if !covers {
            return false;
        }
        match exe_of_window(hwnd) {
            Some(exe) => !FULLSCREEN_EXEMPT.contains(&exe.as_str()),
            None => true,
        }
    }
}

cat > timer.rs <<'EOF'
//! [`crate::animator::Backend`] for real Windows hardware: a high-resolution
//! waitable timer for frame pacing, `QueryPerformanceCounter` for the clock,
//! and the refresh rate of the monitor under the cursor for
//! `frame_rate = "auto"` (cached, so it is not re-queried every frame).

use super::inject;
use crate::animator::Backend;
use crate::engine::Output;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};
use windows::core::PCWSTR;
use windows::Win32::Foundation::{CloseHandle, HANDLE, POINT};
use windows::Win32::Graphics::Gdi::{
    EnumDisplaySettingsW, GetMonitorInfoW, MonitorFromPoint, DEVMODEW, ENUM_CURRENT_SETTINGS,
    MONITORINFO, MONITORINFOEXW, MONITOR_DEFAULTTONEAREST,
};
use windows::Win32::Media::{timeBeginPeriod, timeEndPeriod};
use windows::Win32::System::Performance::{QueryPerformanceCounter, QueryPerformanceFrequency};
use windows::Win32::System::Threading::{
    CreateWaitableTimerExW, GetCurrentThread, SetThreadPriority, SetWaitableTimer,
    WaitForSingleObject, CREATE_WAITABLE_TIMER_HIGH_RESOLUTION, THREAD_PRIORITY_ABOVE_NORMAL,
    TIMER_ALL_ACCESS,
};
use windows::Win32::UI::WindowsAndMessaging::GetCursorPos;

const DEFAULT_HZ: u32 = 60;
/// The cursor may move to another monitor; re-check the refresh rate this
/// often while animating (cheap: a few syscalls, twice a second).
const HZ_TTL: Duration = Duration::from_millis(500);

/// Raise the calling thread slightly above normal so frame pacing and the
/// hook callback stay responsive under load (best-effort).
pub fn boost_thread_priority() {
    // SAFETY: operates on the current thread's pseudo-handle.
    unsafe {
        let _ = SetThreadPriority(GetCurrentThread(), THREAD_PRIORITY_ABOVE_NORMAL);
    }
}

fn query_refresh_hz() -> Option<u32> {
    // SAFETY: every out-structure is a correctly sized, zero-initialised
    // local; `szDevice` is a NUL-terminated buffer that `GetMonitorInfoW`
    // fills and that outlives the `EnumDisplaySettingsW` call.
    unsafe {
        let mut point = POINT::default();
        GetCursorPos(&mut point).ok()?;
        let monitor = MonitorFromPoint(point, MONITOR_DEFAULTTONEAREST);
        let mut info = MONITORINFOEXW::default();
        info.monitorInfo.cbSize = std::mem::size_of::<MONITORINFOEXW>() as u32;
        if !GetMonitorInfoW(monitor, (&mut info as *mut MONITORINFOEXW).cast::<MONITORINFO>()).as_bool() {
            return None;
        }
        let mut mode = DEVMODEW {
            dmSize: std::mem::size_of::<DEVMODEW>() as u16,
            ..Default::default()
        };
        if !EnumDisplaySettingsW(PCWSTR(info.szDevice.as_ptr()), ENUM_CURRENT_SETTINGS, &mut mode).as_bool() {
            return None;
        }
        // 0 / 1 mean "hardware default" in `dmDisplayFrequency`.
        (mode.dmDisplayFrequency >= 30).then_some(mode.dmDisplayFrequency)
    }
}

pub struct WindowsBackend {
    qpc_freq: i64,
    timer: Option<HANDLE>,
    /// `timeBeginPeriod(1)` is process-wide and must be paired 1:1 with
    /// `timeEndPeriod(1)`; this tracks whether we currently hold it.
    fine_timer_period: bool,
    hz_cache: Option<(u32, Instant)>,
}

impl WindowsBackend {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        let mut freq = 0i64;
        // SAFETY: out-pointer to a stack local; always succeeds on Windows
        // XP+ per the documented contract.
        unsafe {
            let _ = QueryPerformanceFrequency(&mut freq);
        }
        WindowsBackend {
            qpc_freq: freq.max(1),
            timer: None,
            fine_timer_period: false,
            hz_cache: None,
        }
    }

    fn ensure_timer(&mut self) {
        if self.timer.is_some() || self.fine_timer_period {
            return; // already set up (or already on the sleep fallback)
        }
        // SAFETY: standard `CreateWaitableTimerExW` call; the returned
        // handle (if any) is owned by `self` and closed in `release`.
        let handle = unsafe {
            CreateWaitableTimerExW(None, None, CREATE_WAITABLE_TIMER_HIGH_RESOLUTION, TIMER_ALL_ACCESS.0)
        };
        if let Ok(h) = handle {
            self.timer = Some(h);
        } else {
            // Fallback path (Windows older than 10 1803): 1 ms scheduler
            // granularity makes `std::thread::sleep` usable.
            // SAFETY: `timeBeginPeriod` has no preconditions; paired with
            // `timeEndPeriod` in `release`.
            unsafe {
                let _ = timeBeginPeriod(1);
            }
            self.fine_timer_period = true;
        }
    }
}

impl Default for WindowsBackend {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

impl Backend for WindowsBackend {
    fn now_us(&mut self) -> u64 {
        let mut ticks = 0i64;
        // SAFETY: out-pointer to a stack local.
        unsafe {
            let _ = QueryPerformanceCounter(&mut ticks);
        }
        // i128 avoids overflow for large uptimes at microsecond precision.
        ((i128::from(ticks) * 1_000_000) / i128::from(self.qpc_freq)).max(0) as u64
    }

    fn refresh_hz(&mut self) -> u32 {
        if let Some((hz, at)) = self.hz_cache {
            if at.elapsed() < HZ_TTL {
                return hz;
            }
        }
        let hz = query_refresh_hz().unwrap_or(DEFAULT_HZ);
        self.hz_cache = Some((hz, Instant::now()));
        hz
    }

    fn wait_frame(&mut self, hz: u32) {
        self.ensure_timer();
        let period_100ns = -((1_000_000_000 / 100) / i64::from(hz.max(1)));
        if let Some(timer) = self.timer {
            // SAFETY: `timer` is a live waitable-timer handle owned by
            // `self`; `period_100ns` is a valid relative (negative) offset.
            unsafe {
                if SetWaitableTimer(timer, &period_100ns, 0, None, None, false).is_ok() {
                    WaitForSingleObject(timer, u32::MAX);
                    return;
                }
            }
        }
        // Fallback: plain sleep (coarser, but never blocks forever).
        std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_micros(1_000_000 / u64::from(hz.max(1))));
    }

    fn send(&mut self, out: Output) {
        inject::send_wheel(out);
    }

    fn release(&mut self) {
        self.hz_cache = None;
        if let Some(timer) = self.timer.take() {
            // SAFETY: `timer` was created by us in `ensure_timer` and is
            // not used anywhere else; closing it once is required by NFR
            // "nothing held when idle".
            unsafe {
                let _ = CloseHandle(timer);
            }
        }
        if self.fine_timer_period {
            // SAFETY: pairs the `timeBeginPeriod(1)` above exactly once.
            unsafe {
                let _ = timeEndPeriod(1);
            }
            self.fine_timer_period = false;
        }
    }
}
